Repo path -> canonical path (bytes). Canonical -> repo; edit the canonical file first. Canonical root `claude-agentsrv:/home/jhan/workspace/titan/` -> `artifacts/titan/` (the same mapping the 2026-11-02 Codex batch above uses); `reference-scratch/` mirrors `/scratch/` and `review-scratch/` mirrors `/tmp/` as that batch registered them.
